<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * Virtualized data table: 10k+ rows render only what is on screen. Headers sort</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * on click (asc → desc → off), drag to reorder (Alt+←/→ from the keyboard) and</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> */</span></span>
